## Project Description

Derivatives. Structurally: 27 project-authored contract(s) across 3 chain(s); 1 ERC20 token, 1 Chainlink feed; role-gated via AccessControl; upgradeable via ERC1967/UUPS proxies; built on openzeppelin.

### Architecture

The protocol comprises 7 functional families. Its contracts share 7 common project-authored base contract(s) (storage, safeownableupgradeable, admin). Dominant framework: openzeppelin.
